declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t298 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/asyncwrite.will$main.task"(i32 -2)
  %t300 = alloca i32
  %t299 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t298, ptr %t300, ptr null)
  %t301 = icmp ne i32 %t299, 0
  br i1 %t301, label %main.async.fail.37, label %main.async.ok.37
main.async.fail.37:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.37:
  %t302 = load i32, ptr %t300
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t302
}
